PREFACE 


This is the second volume of the Catalogue, of Arabic Manuscripts of 
Raza Library^ Rampur which is published under the auspices of Ministry of 
Educatioii, Government of India. 

The MSS. described in the volume belong to the head of Prayers, 
Theology and Polemics. They are 1121 in number and are bound in 767 
volumes. 

The compiler, Mr. Imtiyaz 'AH ‘Arshi, has applied in this volume the 
same method which was.adopted by him in the previous one, and I trusf that 
the high standard of scholarship reached in the 1st volume has been success- 
fully maintained by him in the present one al$p. 

Among the rare manuscripts cataloged in this volume, the following 
deserve particular attention : — 

1. The author’s copy .of al-Futuhatu’r-Rabbaniyyah by M. ‘A. b. M. 
•Allan b. lbr. al-Bakrl <d. 1057/1647). No. 1243. 

2. The unique copy of a commentary on Mulla'All Qari’s al-tf izbu’l- 
A‘zam by Maq$ud b. ‘Abdi’l-Khalii}. No. 1281. 

3. The unique copy of Jawami u'l-Khairat by Jafoalii'd-Din Abu 
’Umar Maljmud b. M. b. *A. a’sh-Shafi‘I al-Qadirl. No. 1348. 

4. The unique copy of al-Jawahiru'l-Mudi'ah fi Hilytjti Khairt’l- 
Bariyyah by Shah Waliyyu'llah a'd-Dihlawi. No. 1355. 

5. A very valuable copy of a’$-§abifatu*l-Kami)ah by al-Imdra 
Zainu’l-* Abidin (d. 94/712), with the autograph of M. TaqI al-Majlisi (d. 
1070/1659). No. 1430. 

6. The unique copy of Aftjalu’l-Fiqh by al-ImSm AbG Hanifah al- 
K6fl (d. 1 50/767). No. 1 5 1 1 . 

7. SharbU' Khula$ati’l-U$ul of Abfl Man$Gr al-Maturldi *(d. 333/944) 
by an anonymous author, composed for Kh9ni A‘zam ^bu’l-Muzaffar Firfiz 
Khan. No. 1514. 

8. Sharfyu U§uli 1‘tiqadi Ahli’s Sunnah by Abu*l*Q£sim Hibatullah b. 
y. b. Man$Qr a’t-Tabari a’r-Razi al-Lalka’i (d. 418/1027). No. 1531. 

9. al-Muyassir fiT-Kalfim by Abu’l-ftasan ‘All b. M. b. flu. al-Baz- 
dawi al-flanaff, Fakhru’l-IslSm (d. 482/1089). No. 1536.

10. The unique copy of gubdatu’L'Aq&'id, a commentary on ‘Aqft’id-

I add my appreciation of the hard work and devotion of the compiler 
to his work, hoping thjt in his good hands the preparation of the next volumes 
of the Catalogue will progress rapidly, so that the literary treasure at 
Raza Library can be utilised even better by the scholars all over the world.

the above text composed 
in 768/ 1 367. Written in 
Samarqand with notes in 
the margins and on frag- 
ments of paper pasted 
here and there.

gold and coloured Jidwal 
& red Banka; and with 
notes in the margins. Bears 
the signature of Jalalu’d- 
Din Gaidar Mirathi dated 
1837.

gold & coloured Jidwal.

A signature dated 1064/ 
1653-4 is borne on fol. la.
